[00:21:08.632] Let the
[00:21:08.912] record
[00:21:18.012] reflect that this is a public hearing that is being held pursuant to the
[00:21:22.032] requirements of Section 147F of the
[00:21:25.652] Internal Revenue Code of 1986, as amended.
[00:21:29.552] Notice of this public hearing was published on the July 6th,
[00:21:33.812] 2026 website of the Office of the City
[00:21:37.612] Clerk of the City of Chicago. Let the reporter mark
[00:21:41.461] the screenshot of such notice as committee exhibit number one
[00:21:45.532] for identification.
[00:21:49.532] This is a hearing regarding a plan to finance to issue the
[00:21:53.432] multifamily housing revenue note, series
[00:21:56.752] 2026, for the Levy House redevelopment project, in a
[00:22:00.492] principal amount not to exceed $15 million.
[00:22:04.322] We'll refer to this as the note. The proceeds
[00:22:08.232] of the note will be used by Levy House Preservation
[00:22:11.632] Associates Limited Partnership, an Illinois limited
[00:22:15.712] partnership, we will refer to them as the borrower, to
[00:22:19.532] finance an affordable housing development project by
[00:22:23.232] acquiring and rehabilitating a low-income,
[00:22:26.512] multifamily housing development project, which we will
[00:22:30.052] refer to as the project.
[00:22:33.091] This project consists of, A, the acquisition and
[00:22:36.032] rehabilitation of an eight-story building located
[00:22:39.352] generally at 1221 West Sherwin Avenue,
[00:22:43.852] we will refer to this as the building, which will contain
[00:22:47.772] 57 residential rental dwelling units, comprised
[00:22:51.692] of 56
[00:22:53.592] one-bedroom units and one two-bedroom unit.
[00:22:57.972] And B, paying fees, expenses, and costs
[00:23:01.432] incurred in the connection with the authorization,
[00:23:04.832] issuance, and sale of the note.
[00:23:07.792] Of the 57 residential rental units in this project, at
[00:23:11.752] least 23 units will be held available to
[00:23:15.232] households earning up to 60% of the area median
[00:23:19.092] income.
[00:23:20.512] The borrower will acquire the building from POA Levy
[00:23:24.372] House LLC, and thereafter will own the
[00:23:27.832] project. POA Levy House GP
[00:23:31.312] LLC, an Illinois limited liability company,
[00:23:35.132] serves as the general partner of the borrower.
[00:23:39.352] The city will issue the note pursuant to its powers as a home
[00:23:43.192] rule unit of government under the 1970 Constitution

[01:16:05.258] The officers arrested both men and transported them to Area Four for
[01:16:08.878] questioning. At Area Four, Simon and Bentazos
